<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-6A4D3A4B-ED56-4425-A042-95E7B753ADB3'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Jonathon and his dad were both born on $5 \text{th}$ August.

Jonathon was born in $1985$ and his father is $32$ years older than him. 

In which year was his dad born?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Year dad was born}    &= 1985 - 32  \\\\
                            &= 1953 
\end{aligned}
$


</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1953$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Year dad was born}    &= 1985 - 32  \\\\
                            &= 1953 
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$1953$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-B42D6744-B5FF-4E7F-A4E1-0E475C841546'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Hasan wakes up at the time shown below.

He needs to be at cricket training at $10 \ \text{am}$ - how many minutes does he have until his training starts?

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)


</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time left}              &= 10{:}00 - 8{:}15  \\\\
                              &= 1 \ \text{hour} \ 45 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                              &= 1 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 45  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                              &= 60 + 45  \\\\
                              &= 105  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$105  \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time left}              &= 10{:}00 - 8{:}15  \\\\
                              &= 1 \ \text{hour} \ 45 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                              &= 1 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 45  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                              &= 60 + 45  \\\\
                              &= 105  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$105  \ \text{minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

Mrs Green parked her car in a car park at $9.00 \ \text{am}$. She drove out of the car park at $5.00 \ \text{pm}$.

Car Parking Charges: $\pounds 1.80$ per hour 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

For how many hours was her car in the car park?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of hours}    &=  5.00 \ \text{pm} - 9.00 \ \text{am} \\\\
                          &=  8 \ \text{hours} 
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$8 \ \text{hours}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of hours}    &=  5.00 \ \text{pm} - 9.00 \ \text{am} \\\\
                          &=  8 \ \text{hours} 
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$8 \ \text{hours}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

How much did she pay, in total, for parking her car?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Parking charges}  &=  8 \times 1.80 \\\\
                        &=  \pounds 14.40 
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 14.40$

</div>

<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-DDD0AC6B-3CD4-48A3-A86A-6AEE1949B832'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The students on the Aldenham Geography trip to Iceland arrive at Heathrow Airport at $10{:}45$.
Their flight leaves at $13{:}05$.

How many minutes do they have to wait at the airport before their flight leaves?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Waiting Time}           &= 13{:}05 - 10{:}45 \\\\
                              &= 2 \ \text{hour} \ 20 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                              &= 2 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 20  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                              &= 120 + 20  \\\\
                              &= 140  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$140  \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Waiting Time}           &= 13{:}05 - 10{:}45 \\\\
                              &= 2 \ \text{hour} \ 20 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                              &= 2 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 20  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                              &= 120 + 20  \\\\
                              &= 140  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$140  \ \text{minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-0B2DD2B6-B549-4742-8C8B-E59718ECB00B'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

My train left Hocksworth at $8.20$ and arrived in Abbleshem at $10.50$ later that morning.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

How long in minutes did the journey take?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Time taken}            &= 10.50 - 8.20 \\\\
                              &= 2 \ \text {hour} \ 30 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                              &= 2 \times 60  \ \text {minutes} + 30 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                              &= 120 + 30 \\\\
                              &= 150 \ \text {minutes}
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$150 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Time taken}            &= 10.50 - 8.20 \\\\
                              &= 2 \ \text {hour} \ 30 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                              &= 2 \times 60  \ \text {minutes} + 30 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                              &= 120 + 30 \\\\
                              &= 150 \ \text {minutes}
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$150 \ \text{minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Given that Abbleshem is $170 \ \text{miles}$ away from Hocksworth, what was the average speed of my train?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text {Speed} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Time}}$


ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required
. The time is calculated in minutes, but the final speed has to be in miles per hour.

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Speed}    &= \dfrac {170 \ \text {miles}} {150 \ \text {minutes}} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac {170 \ \text {miles}} {\dfrac {150}{60} \ \text {hour}} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac {170 \times 60} {150} \ \text {miles/hour} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac {17 \times 10 \times 15 \times 4} {15 \times 10} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac {17 \times \cancel{10} \times \cancel{15} \times 4} {\cancel{15} \times \cancel{10}} \\\\
                 &= 17 \times 4 \\\\
                 &= 68 \ \text {miles/hour}
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$68 \ \text {miles/hour}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_formula_speed
<emptyline>
rtq_abbr_md_unit_no_dot_note. The time is calculated in minutes, but the final speed has to be in miles per hour.
<emptyline>
$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Speed}    &= \dfrac {170 \ \text {miles}} {150 \ \text {minutes}} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac {170 \ \text {miles}} {\dfrac {150}{60} \ \text {hour}} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac {170 \times 60} {150} \ \text {miles/hour} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac {17 \times 10 \times 15 \times 4} {15 \times 10} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac {17 \times \cancel{10} \times \cancel{15} \times 4} {\cancel{15} \times \cancel{10}} \\\\
                 &= 17 \times 4 \\\\
                 &= 68 \ \text {miles/hour}
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$68 \ \text {miles/hour}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
</ul>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-652278E2-8C43-4EAE-A78E-26EF36905775'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The students on the Aldenham Football Tour to Madrid arrive at Heathrow Airport at $05{:}35$.

Their flight leaves at $08{:}10$.

How many minutes do they have to wait at the airport before their flight leaves?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Wait at the airport}     &= 08{:}10 - 05{:}35 \\\\
                                &= 2 \ \text{hour} \ 35 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                                &= 2 \times 60 \ \text {minutes} + 35 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                                &= 120 + 35 \\\\
                                &= 155 \ \text{minutes}
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$155 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Wait at the airport}     &= 08{:}10 - 05{:}35 \\\\
                                &= 2 \ \text{hour} \ 35 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                                &= 2 \times 60 \ \text {minutes} + 35 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                                &= 120 + 35 \\\\
                                &= 155 \ \text{minutes}
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$155 \ \text{minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-E910C2F9-6456-4BBC-AFDD-535BDC142506'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Freya’s bus leaves at $08{:}05$. She arrives $12 \ \text{minutes}$ early 
for the bus. When does she arrive?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Arrival Time}  &= 8 {:} 05 - 12 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                     &= 7 {:} 58
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$7 {:} 58$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Arrival Time}  &= 8 {:} 05 - 12 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                     &= 7 {:} 58
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$7 {:} 58$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-FF8DD05C-B551-450C-9DFF-1AF6454C83A6'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

How many minutes are there in three and a half hours?

-  $4$ 
-  $60$ 
-  $210$ 
-  $180.5$ 
-  $3600$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 3 \text{ hours} + 30 \text{ minutes} \\\\
&= 3 \times 60 \text{ minutes} + 30 \text{ minutes}\\\\
&= 180 + 30 \\\\
&= 210 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$210 \text{ minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 3 \text{ hours} + 30 \text{ minutes} \\\\
&= 3 \times 60 \text{ minutes} + 30 \text{ minutes}\\\\
&= 180 + 30 \\\\
&= 210 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$210 \text{ minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-D1E91C2C-7808-4EE0-BABC-FC7615F5E3D4'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

How long is it, in hours and minutes, between $09{:}34$ and $13{:}21$?

-  $4 \ \text{hrs} 57 \ \text{mins}$ 
-  $3 \ \text{hrs} 47 \ \text{mins}$ 
-  $4 \ \text{hrs} 37 \ \text{mins}$
-  $22 \ \text{hrs} 55 \ \text{mins}$ 
-  $4 \ \text{hrs} 13 \ \text{mins}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Difference}   &= 13{:}21 - 9{:}34 \\\\
                     &= 4 \ \text {hours} \ 47 \ \text {minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$ 4 \ \text{hours} \ 47 \ \text {minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Difference}   &= 13{:}21 - 9{:}34 \\\\
                     &= 4 \ \text {hours} \ 47 \ \text {minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$ 4 \ \text{hours} \ 47 \ \text {minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

MULTIPLE CHOICE

Circle the correct answer to the questions in this section 

How many seconds are there in four minutes?

-  $4$ 
-  $60$ 
-  $460$ 
-  $240$
-  $3600$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 4 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= 4 \times 60 \ \text {seconds} \\\\
&= 240 \ \text {seconds}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='option'>
<p>D</p>
</div>
<div class='answer'>

$240 \ \text {seconds}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 4 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= 4 \times 60 \ \text {seconds} \\\\
&= 240 \ \text {seconds}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>D</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$240 \ \text {seconds}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-FDDBCC32-B9DD-47A9-8DE7-22DB6C82700E'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

How long is it, in hours and minutes, between $10{:}28$ and $16{:}06$?

-  $5 \ \text{hrs} \ 38 \ \text{mins}$ 
-  $8 \ \text{hrs} \ 34 \ \text{mins}$ 
-  $5 \ \text{hrs} \ 36 \ \text{mins}$ 
-  $26 \ \text{hrs} \ 34 \ \text{mins}$ 
-  $4 \ \text{hrs} \ 33 \ \text{mins}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 16{:}06 - 10{:}28 \\\\
&= 5 \ \text{hrs} \ 38 \ \text{mins}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='option'>
<p>A</p>
</div>
<div class='answer'>

$5 \ \text{hrs} \ 38 \ \text{mins}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 16{:}06 - 10{:}28 \\\\
&= 5 \ \text{hrs} \ 38 \ \text{mins}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>A</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$5 \ \text{hrs} \ 38 \ \text{mins}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-36053732-A8EE-4973-8B6C-1D0E4290AC4D'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>


Last week George spent $12 \ \text{hours}$ playing on his Xbox. 

He spent the same length of time on it each weekday and twice as long on it each day at the weekend. 

How long (in hours and minutes) did he send on his Xbox on Saturday?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the time spent playing each weekday $= a$

Time spent playing on weekend $= 2a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
5 \times a + 2 \times 2a         &= 12  \\\\
5a + 4a                          &= 12 \\\\
9a                               &= 12 \\\\
a                                &= \dfrac {12} {9} \\\\
a                                &= \dfrac {3 \times 4} {3 \times 3} \\\\
a                                &= \dfrac {\cancel{3} \times 4} {\cancel{3} \times 3} \\\\
a                                &= \dfrac {4} {3} \ \text {hours}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time spent on saturday}    &= 2a  \\\\
                                 &= 2 \times \dfrac {4} {3} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac {8} {3} \\\\
                                 &= 2 \dfrac {2} {3} \ \text {hours} \\\\
                                 &= 2 \ \text {hours } 40 \ \text {minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$2 \ \text {hours } 40 \ \text {minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the time spent playing each weekday $= a$
<emptyline>
Time spent playing on weekend $= 2a$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
5 \times a + 2 \times 2a         &= 12  \\\\
5a + 4a                          &= 12 \\\\
9a                               &= 12 \\\\
a                                &= \dfrac {12} {9} \\\\
a                                &= \dfrac {3 \times 4} {3 \times 3} \\\\
a                                &= \dfrac {\cancel{3} \times 4} {\cancel{3} \times 3} \\\\
a                                &= \dfrac {4} {3} \ \text {hours}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time spent on saturday}    &= 2a  \\\\
                                 &= 2 \times \dfrac {4} {3} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac {8} {3} \\\\
                                 &= 2 \dfrac {2} {3} \ \text {hours} \\\\
                                 &= 2 \ \text {hours } 40 \ \text {minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$2 \ \text {hours } 40 \ \text {minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-7DED225D-BE66-49EA-A9DA-91175FD26034'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

It is $165 \ \text{minutes}$ before midnight. 
Put one digit into each of the four boxes to show the time on this $24$-hour digital clock.


$
\huge {
\boxed {\phantom{2}} \ 
\boxed {\phantom{2}}
{:} 
\boxed {\phantom{1}} \ 
\boxed {\phantom{3}}
}
$


</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 24{:}00 - 165 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
&= 24{:}00 - 120 \ \text {minutes} - 45 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
&= 24{:}00 - 2 \ \text {hours} \ 45 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
&= 21{:}15
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$21{:}15$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 24{:}00 - 165 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
&= 24{:}00 - 120 \ \text {minutes} - 45 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
&= 24{:}00 - 2 \ \text {hours} \ 45 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
&= 21{:}15
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$21{:}15$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Freya puts a casserole in the oven at $11.40 \ \text{am}$.

It needs $1 \ \text{hour}$ and $45 \ \text{minutes}$ to cook.

At what time will it be cooked?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time} &= 11{:}40 \ \text{am} + 1 \ \text{hours } 45 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
            &= 1{:}25 \ \text{pm} 
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1{:}25 \ \text{pm}$ 

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time} &= 11{:}40 \ \text{am} + 1 \ \text{hours } 45 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
            &= 1{:}25 \ \text{pm} 
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$1{:}25 \ \text{pm}$ 
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

How many minutes are there from $11.11$ util $23.23$ on the same day?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 23{:}23 - 11{:}11 \\\\
&= 12 \text{ hours} \ 12 \text{ minutes} \\\\
&= 12 \times 60 \text{ minutes} + 12\text{ minutes} \\\\
&= 720 + 12 \\\\
&= 732 \text { minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$732 \text { minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 23{:}23 - 11{:}11 \\\\
&= 12 \text{ hours} \ 12 \text{ minutes} \\\\
&= 12 \times 60 \text{ minutes} + 12\text{ minutes} \\\\
&= 720 + 12 \\\\
&= 732 \text { minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$732 \text { minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Archie is $20$ years, $20$ months, $20$ weeks and $20$ days old.

What age will he be on his next birthday?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>


$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Archie's current age}              &= 20 \text{ years} + 20 \text{ months} + 20 \text{ weeks} + 20 \text{ days} \\\\
                                          &= 20 \text{ years} + 20 \text{ months} + 22 \text{ weeks} + 6  \text{ days} \\\\
                                          &= 20 \text{ years} + 25 \text{ months} + 2  \text{ weeks} + 6  \text{ days} \\\\
                                          &= 22 \text{ years}, 1 \text{ month}, 2 \text{ weeks}, 6 \text{ days} \\\\
\text {Archie's age on his next birthday} &= 22 \text{ years}, 1 \text{ month}, 2 \text{ weeks}, 6 \text{ days} + 1 \text{ year} \\\\
                                          &= 23 \text{ years}, 1 \text{ month}, 2 \text{ weeks}, 6 \text{ days} 
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$23 \text{ years}, 1 \text{ month}, 2 \text{ weeks}, 6 \text{ days}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Archie's current age}              &= 20 \text{ years} + 20 \text{ months} + 20 \text{ weeks} + 20 \text{ days} \\\\
                                          &= 20 \text{ years} + 20 \text{ months} + 22 \text{ weeks} + 6  \text{ days} \\\\
                                          &= 20 \text{ years} + 25 \text{ months} + 2  \text{ weeks} + 6  \text{ days} \\\\
                                          &= 22 \text{ years}, 1 \text{ month}, 2 \text{ weeks}, 6 \text{ days} \\\\
\text {Archie's age on his next birthday} &= 22 \text{ years}, 1 \text{ month}, 2 \text{ weeks}, 6 \text{ days} + 1 \text{ year} \\\\
                                          &= 23 \text{ years}, 1 \text{ month}, 2 \text{ weeks}, 6 \text{ days} 
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$23 \text{ years}, 1 \text{ month}, 2 \text{ weeks}, 6 \text{ days}$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Gemma had a baby born on $1 \text{st}$ February $2017$.

On what date will the baby be $400$ days old?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Date}     &= 1 \text{st February } 2017 + 400 \ \text{ days} \\\\
                &= 1 \text{st February } 2017 + 365 \ \text{ days} + 28 \ \text{ days} + 7 \ \text{ days}  \\\\
                &= 1 \text{st February } 2017 + 1 \  \text{year} + 1 \ \text{month} + 7 \ \text{ days}  \\\\
                &= 8 \text{th March } 2018
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$8 \text{th March } 2018$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Date}     &= 1 \text{st February } 2017 + 400 \ \text{ days} \\\\
                &= 1 \text{st February } 2017 + 365 \ \text{ days} + 28 \ \text{ days} + 7 \ \text{ days}  \\\\
                &= 1 \text{st February } 2017 + 1 \  \text{year} + 1 \ \text{month} + 7 \ \text{ days}  \\\\
                &= 8 \text{th March } 2018
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$8 \text{th March } 2018$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Freya puts a casserole in the oven at $11.40 \ \text{am}$. 

It needs $1 \ \text{hour}$ and $45 \ \text{minutes}$ to cook.

At what time will it be cooked?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time} &= 11{:}40 \ \text{am} + 1 \ \text{hours } 45 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
            &= 1{:}25 \ \text{pm} 
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1{:}25 \ \text{pm}$ 

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time} &= 11{:}40 \ \text{am} + 1 \ \text{hours } 45 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
            &= 1{:}25 \ \text{pm} 
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$1{:}25 \ \text{pm}$ 
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

How many minutes are there from $11.11$ until $23.23$ on the same day?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 23{:}23 - 11{:}11 \\\\
&= 12 \text{ hours} \ 12 \text{ minutes} \\\\
&= 12 \times 60 \text{ minutes} + 12\text{ minutes} \\\\
&= 720 + 12 \\\\
&= 732 \text { minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$732 \text { minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 23{:}23 - 11{:}11 \\\\
&= 12 \text{ hours} \ 12 \text{ minutes} \\\\
&= 12 \times 60 \text{ minutes} + 12\text{ minutes} \\\\
&= 720 + 12 \\\\
&= 732 \text { minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$732 \text { minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Archie is $20$ years, $20$ months, $20$ weeks and $20$ days old. 

What age will he be on his next birthday?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Archie's current age}              &= 20 \text{ years} + 20 \text{ months} + 20 \text{ weeks} + 20 \text{ days} \\\\
                                          &= 20 \text{ years} + 20 \text{ months} + 22 \text{ weeks} + 6  \text{ days} \\\\
                                          &= 20 \text{ years} + 25 \text{ months} + 2  \text{ weeks} + 6  \text{ days} \\\\
                                          &= 22 \text{ years}, 1 \text{ month}, 2 \text{ weeks}, 6 \text{ days} \\\\
\text {Archie's age on his next birthday} &= 22 \text{ years}, 1 \text{ month}, 2 \text{ weeks}, 6 \text{ days} + 1 \text{ year} \\\\
                                          &= 23 \text{ years}, 1 \text{ month}, 2 \text{ weeks}, 6 \text{ days} 
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$23 \text{ years} \ 1 \text{ months} \ 2 \text{ weeks} \ 6 \text{ days} $

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Archie's current age}              &= 20 \text{ years} + 20 \text{ months} + 20 \text{ weeks} + 20 \text{ days} \\\\
                                          &= 20 \text{ years} + 20 \text{ months} + 22 \text{ weeks} + 6  \text{ days} \\\\
                                          &= 20 \text{ years} + 25 \text{ months} + 2  \text{ weeks} + 6  \text{ days} \\\\
                                          &= 22 \text{ years}, 1 \text{ month}, 2 \text{ weeks}, 6 \text{ days} \\\\
\text {Archie's age on his next birthday} &= 22 \text{ years}, 1 \text{ month}, 2 \text{ weeks}, 6 \text{ days} + 1 \text{ year} \\\\
                                          &= 23 \text{ years}, 1 \text{ month}, 2 \text{ weeks}, 6 \text{ days} 
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$23 \text{ years} \ 1 \text{ months} \ 2 \text{ weeks} \ 6 \text{ days} $
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Gemma had a baby born on $1 \text{st}$ February $2017$. 

On what date will the baby be $400$ days old?


</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Date}     &= 1 \text{st February } 2017 + 400 \ \text{ days} \\\\
                &= 1 \text{st February } 2017 + 365 \ \text{ days} + 28 \ \text{ days} + 7 \ \text{ days}  \\\\
                &= 1 \text{st February } 2017 + 1 \  \text{year} + 1 \ \text{month} + 7 \ \text{ days}  \\\\
                &= 8 \text{th March } 2018
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$8 \text{th March } 2018$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Date}     &= 1 \text{st February } 2017 + 400 \ \text{ days} \\\\
                &= 1 \text{st February } 2017 + 365 \ \text{ days} + 28 \ \text{ days} + 7 \ \text{ days}  \\\\
                &= 1 \text{st February } 2017 + 1 \  \text{year} + 1 \ \text{month} + 7 \ \text{ days}  \\\\
                &= 8 \text{th March } 2018
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$8 \text{th March } 2018$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>

<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

A bus holds $12$ people.

How many buses are needed to transport $175$ students to the athletics stadium?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Buses needed } &= 175 \div 12 \\\\
                      &= 14 \ \text {remainder} \ 7 \\\\
                      &\approx 15
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$15$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Buses needed } &= 175 \div 12 \\\\
                      &= 14 \ \text {remainder} \ 7 \\\\
                      &\approx 15
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$15$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Mark goes to school from Monday to Friday, but he has trouble getting out 
of bed and arrives late if there is an **‘s’** in the name of the day. 

During the $12$ weeks of term, how many days did he arrive late?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Count of days that have 's'}  &= 3 \\\\
\text {Days he arrive late }        &= 12 \times 3 \\\\
                                    &= 36
\end{aligned}
$


</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$36$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Count of days that have 's'}  &= 3 \\\\
\text {Days he arrive late }        &= 12 \times 3 \\\\
                                    &= 36
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$36$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Cormac leaves the house with $\pounds 120$ in his wallet after his birthday.

He spends $25 \%$ of it on a game for his Xbox, and $10 \%$ of the rest on a DVD. 
How much money does he have left?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Money spent on Xbox} &= 25 \% \ \text {of } 120 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {25} {100} \times 120 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {25 \times 120} {100} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {25 \times 30 \times 4} {25 \times 4} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac { \cancel 25 \times 30 \times \cancel 4} {\cancel 25 \times \cancel 4} \\\\
                            &= \pounds  30 \\\\
\text {Money spent on DVD}  &= 10 \% \ \text {of } (120 - 90) \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {10} {100} \times 90 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {10 \times 90} {100} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {1\cancel{0} \times 9\cancel{0}} {1\cancel{0}\cancel{0}} \\\\
                            &= \pounds 9 \\\\
\text {Money left}          &= 120 - 30 - 9 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 71
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 72$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Money spent on Xbox} &= 25 \% \ \text {of } 120 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {25} {100} \times 120 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {25 \times 120} {100} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {25 \times 30 \times 4} {25 \times 4} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac { \cancel 25 \times 30 \times \cancel 4} {\cancel 25 \times \cancel 4} \\\\
                            &= \pounds  30 \\\\
\text {Money spent on DVD}  &= 10 \% \ \text {of } (120 - 90) \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {10} {100} \times 90 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {10 \times 90} {100} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {1\cancel{0} \times 9\cancel{0}} {1\cancel{0}\cancel{0}} \\\\
                            &= \pounds 9 \\\\
\text {Money left}          &= 120 - 30 - 9 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 71
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 72$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
</ul>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-725486A7-3062-47A5-9B6E-60454F2B945C'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A train left London at $11{:}30$ and arrived in Glasgow at $15{:}50$. 

The train only made $3$ stops, each lasting $5 \ \text{minutes}$.

For how many minutes was the train moving on this journey?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time}  &= 15{:}50 - 11{:}30 - 5 \times 3 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
             &= 4 \ \text{hours} \ 20 \ \text{minutes} - 15 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
             &= 4 \ \text{hours} \ 05 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$4 \ \text{hours} \ 05 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time}  &= 15{:}50 - 11{:}30 - 5 \times 3 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
             &= 4 \ \text{hours} \ 20 \ \text{minutes} - 15 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
             &= 4 \ \text{hours} \ 05 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$4 \ \text{hours} \ 05 \ \text{minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-9E15412C-4661-4C5C-9D9D-3029A1E8FD6A'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Jack runs the $100 \ \text{metres}$ in a time of $15.47 \ \text{seconds}$.
Emily runs the same distance in a time of $13.762 \ \text{seconds}$.

**Estimate** the difference in their times by rounding each number to the nearest whole number and subtracting.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Difference}              &= 15.47 - 13.762 \\\\ 
                                &\approx 15 - 14 \\\\
                                &= 1 \ \text {second}                        
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1 \ \text {second}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Difference}              &= 15.47 - 13.762 \\\\ 
                                &\approx 15 - 14 \\\\
                                &= 1 \ \text {second}                        
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$1 \ \text {second}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-A7936BDD-9D0B-4DE1-9D29-9396E4564A70'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

How many $\text{minutes}$ are there between $9.23\ \text{am}$ and $1.06\ \text{pm}$? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1{:}06 \ \text {pm} - 9{:}23 \ \text {am} \\\\
&= 3 \ \text {hours} \  43 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
&= 3 \times 60 \ \text {minutes} + 43 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
&= 180 + 43 \\\\
&= 223 \ \text {minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$223 \ \text {minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1{:}06 \ \text {pm} - 9{:}23 \ \text {am} \\\\
&= 3 \ \text {hours} \  43 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
&= 3 \times 60 \ \text {minutes} + 43 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
&= 180 + 43 \\\\
&= 223 \ \text {minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$223 \ \text {minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-FF15893E-F772-4830-8CA9-E540E52C0887'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

**For question $\bold{25}$ and $\bold{26}$ use the information provided in this box.**

![missing table](/papers/missing_table.svg)


For how many hours is it open each week?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

| Day                | Opening hours   |
|--------------------|-----------------|
| SAT                | 9 Till 5        |
| SUN                | 1 Till 5        |
| WEDS and FRI       | 2 Till 5        |

Let's calculate the total hours it is open in each week.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total hours}   &= \text {Sat} + \text {Sun} + \text {Wed} + \text {Fri} \\\\
                     &= 8 \ \text {hours}  +  4 \ \text {hours} +  3 \ \text {hours} +  3 \ \text {hours} \\\\
                     &= 18 \ \text{hours}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$18 \text{hours}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>| Day                | Opening hours   |
|--------------------|-----------------|
| SAT                | 9 Till 5        |
| SUN                | 1 Till 5        |
| WEDS and FRI       | 2 Till 5        |
<emptyline>
Let's calculate the total hours it is open in each week.
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total hours}   &= \text {Sat} + \text {Sun} + \text {Wed} + \text {Fri} \\\\
                     &= 8 \ \text {hours}  +  4 \ \text {hours} +  3 \ \text {hours} +  3 \ \text {hours} \\\\
                     &= 18 \ \text{hours}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$18 \text{hours}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-E16C4E97-F68B-47AE-9AB5-9D9CE481082D'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

How many seconds in $24$ hours?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 24 \ \text{hours} \\\\
&=  24 \times 60 \times 60 \ \text{seconds} \\\\
&=  24 \times 3600 \\\\
&= 86400 \ \text {seconds}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$86400  \ \text {seconds}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 24 \ \text{hours} \\\\
&=  24 \times 60 \times 60 \ \text{seconds} \\\\
&=  24 \times 3600 \\\\
&= 86400 \ \text {seconds}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$86400  \ \text {seconds}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-2E9E403F-844C-41B1-8119-E989E5B6E531'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The sun rose at $05{:}43$ and set at $18{:}05$. How many minutes were there between sunrise and sunset?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Difference}                                   &= 18{:}05 - 5{:}43 \\\\
                                                    &= 12 \ \text{hour} \ 22 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                                                    &= 12 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 22 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                                    &= 720 + 22 \\\\
                                                    &= 742 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$742  \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Difference}                                   &= 18{:}05 - 5{:}43 \\\\
                                                    &= 12 \ \text{hour} \ 22 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                                                    &= 12 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 22 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                                    &= 720 + 22 \\\\
                                                    &= 742 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$742  \ \text{minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-6758134C-A670-44FB-BDD8-5A6BEBD3402C'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

How many seconds in $12$ hours?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&=12 \ \text{hours} \\\\
&= 12 \times 60 \times 60 \ \text {seconds} \\\\
&= 12 \times 3600 \\\\
&= 43200 \ \text {seconds}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$43200 \ \text {seconds}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&=12 \ \text{hours} \\\\
&= 12 \times 60 \times 60 \ \text {seconds} \\\\
&= 12 \times 3600 \\\\
&= 43200 \ \text {seconds}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>

</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-AA0A40BF-651B-45E6-A04A-E3ECF12E2B2D'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The sun rose at $05{:}34$ and set at $21{:}15$. How many minutes were there between sunrise and sunset?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Difference}                                   &= 21{:}15 - 5{:}34 \\\\
                                                    &= 15 \ \text{hour} \ 41 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                                                    &= 15 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 41  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                                    &= 900 + 41 \\\\
                                                    &= 941  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$941  \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Difference}                                   &= 21{:}15 - 5{:}34 \\\\
                                                    &= 15 \ \text{hour} \ 41 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                                                    &= 15 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 41  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                                    &= 900 + 41 \\\\
                                                    &= 941  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

How many seconds in an hour?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1 \ \text{hour} \\\\
&= 1 \times 60 \times 60 \ \text{seconds} \\\\
&= 3600 \ \text{seconds}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$3600 \ \text{seconds}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1 \ \text{hour} \\\\
&= 1 \times 60 \times 60 \ \text{seconds} \\\\
&= 3600 \ \text{seconds}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-58963C70-4BF5-4BBC-9722-C4DB92F81077'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The school day starts at $08{:}55$ and finishes at $15{:}20$. How many minutes long is the school day?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{School duration}      &= 15{:}20 - 08{:}55 \\\\
                            &= 6 \ \text{hour} \ 25 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                            &= 6 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 25  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                            &= 360 + 25 \\\\
                            &= 385  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$385  \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{School duration}      &= 15{:}20 - 08{:}55 \\\\
                            &= 6 \ \text{hour} \ 25 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                            &= 6 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 25  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                            &= 360 + 25 \\\\
                            &= 385  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$385  \ \text{minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-9DD640A7-205F-4E19-A2C2-DA3900B8FC8D'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

How many seconds in $24$ hours?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 24 \ \text{hours} \\\\
&= 24 \times 60 \times 60 \ \text {seconds} \\\\
&= 24 \times 3600 \\\\
&= 86400 \ \text {seconds}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$86400 \ \text {seconds}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 24 \ \text{hours} \\\\
&= 24 \times 60 \times 60 \ \text {seconds} \\\\
&= 24 \times 3600 \\\\
&= 86400 \ \text {seconds}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$86400 \ \text {seconds}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-8B3960FC-2E89-4E92-95E6-DB73E2995DE1'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The sun rose at $05{:}37$ and set at $21{:}10$. How many minutes were there between sunrise and sunset?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Difference}                                   &= 21{:}10 - 5{:}37 \\\\
                                                    &= 15 \ \text{hour} \ 33 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                                                    &= 15 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 33  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                                    &= 900 + 33 \\\\
                                                    &= 933  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$933  \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Difference}                                   &= 21{:}10 - 5{:}37 \\\\
                                                    &= 15 \ \text{hour} \ 33 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                                                    &= 15 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 33  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                                    &= 900 + 33 \\\\
                                                    &= 933  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$933  \ \text{minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
